The [Magic Online Championship Series](/en/articles/feature/2009/05-07-magic-online-championship-series) awards invites to the [Magic World Championships](https://web.archive.org/web/20090630000000/https://magic.wizards.com/en/node/642286) as well as a cash tournament for players who qualify through the series. The 2009 Season 3 Championship was held on June 27. Click below to launch the draft viewer to see how they drafted.

[](https://web.archive.org/web/20090630000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mocs2009s3)

### [Launch the Draft Viewer](https://web.archive.org/web/20090630000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mocs2009s3)

Here's how the top 8 matches occurred.

### Finals

**Orgg Ascetic, 2-0** 

CJswoosh23, 2-1 

### Champion

**Orgg Ascetic, 2-0**
